- 你这里写到一个叫 reflection engine，这种复盘提炼是怎么做的？

    1. 是通过什么来运行的呢？是直接调一个模型还是啥？

    1. 他得有一个触发的动作，是谁来触发，然后调用什么东西去执行？你跟他说一下「帮我复盘一下」，他就能帮你做对吧？他具体逻辑是啥？

    1. reflection 帮我改了之后我怎么知道它改的对不对？改了不能用？

    1. 每次运行后进 reflection 流程，会给一些建议做一些修改，那修改之后的动作是啥？是直接上线还是怎么弄？

## 29 Java 手写题 从可恢复状态机到 DAG 调度

**面试回答：** 先写不变量再写循环：一个时刻只有当前 lease 的所有者能推进；批准绑定动作摘要；副作用发出之前意图已经保存；响应不明不自动重放；依赖没成功不释放子任务；取消和期限阻止新动作。下面用纯 JDK 演示这些机制，便于在白板与本地故障测试中讲透。

### 先界定这个教学 Runtime 能保证什么

`DurableRuntime` 处理一个受批准操作，持久化完整状态帧，用单文件锁阻止第二个本地写者。同步方法只解决同一 JVM 对象的竞争；文件锁与 journal 不替代分布式数据库。journal 的 SHA\-256 用于检测损坏，不证明内容来自可信人；目录权限、认证、备份、日志轮换、密钥和跨机复制不在这个小例子中。

`FileChannel.force(true)` 请求强制写出文件数据/元数据，但这里没有通用目录 fsync、跨平台掉电证明或磁盘故障恢复。撕裂尾帧、校验失败与序号错误直接 fail closed，交由受控恢复处理，不猜测哪个动作已发生。此模型使用外部传入的可信时钟值使测试确定；生产 deadline 要区分本机单调耗时与数据库权威租约时钟，不能让客户端自报时间。

审批保存为状态而不是阻塞一个线程。执行前重新检查身份权限、资源动作摘要、有效期、lease、deadline 和预算。示例动作字符串必须由可信服务端规范化，并包含资源 ID、预期版本和重要参数；不对任意 JSON 原文直接散列后声称等价。资源实际提交还需要下游条件写和幂等保障，本例没有发送真实请求。

重启遇到 `DISPATCHED` 进入 `UNKNOWN`。对账确认 `APPLIED` 才记成功，确认 `NOT_APPLIED` 才形成明确未应用结果；没有可信查询时保持未知。本例不自动从未知重试，真实系统若要继续，需要一个新的受授权调度决策，并保留原 operation 的审计链。取消后对账发现已应用，仍应如实保留 succeeded 与 cancellation requested 两个事实。

### 完整文件 `src/DurableRuntime.java`

```Java
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.channels.FileChannel;
import java.nio.channels.FileLock;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.*;

/** Teaching implementation: one local journal, one operation, trusted clock/auth. */
public final class DurableRuntime implements AutoCloseable {
    public enum Phase { PENDING, RUNNING, WAITING_APPROVAL, READY, DISPATCHED,
        UNKNOWN, SUCCEEDED, CANCELLED, FAILED }
    public enum RemoteState { APPLIED, NOT_APPLIED, UNKNOWN }
    public record Auth(String tenant, String actor, boolean mayWrite) {}
    public record View(long sequence, String tenant, String actor, Phase phase,
        long epoch, String owner, long leaseUntil, long deadline, int remaining,
        String operation, String digest, long approvalUntil, boolean cancelled,
        String result) {}
    private final FileChannel channel;
    private final FileLock lock;
    private View state;
    private boolean poisoned;

    public DurableRuntime(Path path, String tenant, String actor,
                          long deadline, int maxAttempts) throws Exception {
        if (tenant.isBlank() || actor.isBlank() || maxAttempts < 1)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid initial state");
        channel = FileChannel.open(path, StandardOpenOption.CREATE,
                StandardOpenOption.READ, StandardOpenOption.WRITE);
        FileLock acquired;
        try { acquired = channel.tryLock(); }
        catch (RuntimeException | IOException e) { channel.close(); throw e; }
        if (acquired == null) { channel.close(); throw new IOException("journal already owned"); }
        lock = acquired;
        try {
            recover();
            if (state == null) {
                state = new View(0, tenant, actor, Phase.PENDING, 0, "", 0,
                        deadline, maxAttempts, "", "", 0, false, "");
                save(state);
            } else if (!state.tenant().equals(tenant) || !state.actor().equals(actor)) {
                throw new SecurityException("journal identity mismatch");
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            try { close(); } catch (IOException cleanup) { e.addSuppressed(cleanup); }
            throw e;
        }
    }
    public synchronized View view() { return state; }
    private static boolean terminal(Phase p) {
        return p == Phase.SUCCEEDED || p == Phase.CANCELLED || p == Phase.FAILED;
    }
    private void auth(Auth a) {
        if (!state.tenant().equals(a.tenant()) || !state.actor().equals(a.actor()) || !a.mayWrite())
            throw new SecurityException("current authorization denied");
    }
    private void live(long now) {
        if (state.cancelled() || now >= state.deadline() || terminal(state.phase()))
            throw new IllegalStateException("task cannot advance");
    }
    private void lease(String owner, long epoch, long now) {
        if (!state.owner().equals(owner) || state.epoch() != epoch || now >= state.leaseUntil())
            throw new IllegalStateException("stale lease");
    }
    private View next(Phase p, long epoch, String owner, long leaseUntil,
                      int remaining, String op, String digest, long approval,
                      boolean cancelled, String result) {
        return new View(state.sequence()+1, state.tenant(), state.actor(), p, epoch,
                owner, leaseUntil, state.deadline(), remaining, op, digest,
                approval, cancelled, result);
    }
    public synchronized long claim(String owner, long now, long leaseMillis) throws Exception {
        live(now);
        if (owner.isBlank() || leaseMillis <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("bad lease");
        boolean eligible = state.phase() == Phase.PENDING || state.phase() == Phase.READY
                || (state.phase() == Phase.RUNNING && now >= state.leaseUntil());
        if (!eligible) throw new IllegalStateException("not claimable");
        long until = Math.min(state.deadline(), Math.addExact(now, leaseMillis));
        save(next(Phase.RUNNING, state.epoch()+1, owner, until, state.remaining(),
                state.operation(), state.digest(), state.approvalUntil(), false, ""));
        return state.epoch();
    }
    public synchronized void heartbeat(String owner, long epoch, long now,
                                       long leaseMillis) throws Exception {
        live(now); lease(owner, epoch, now);
        if (leaseMillis <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("bad lease");
        if (state.phase()!=Phase.RUNNING && state.phase()!=Phase.DISPATCHED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("no active work");
        save(next(state.phase(), epoch, owner,
                Math.min(state.deadline(), Math.addExact(now, leaseMillis)), state.remaining(),
                state.operation(), state.digest(), state.approvalUntil(), false, state.result()));
    }
    public synchronized void requestApproval(String owner, long epoch, long now,
                       String operation, String canonicalAction) throws Exception {
        live(now); lease(owner, epoch, now);
        if (state.phase()!=Phase.RUNNING || operation.isBlank() || canonicalAction.isBlank())
            throw new IllegalStateException("invalid proposal");
        // Waiting is durable; it does not retain a worker or a thread.
        save(next(Phase.WAITING_APPROVAL, epoch, "", 0, state.remaining(), operation,
                sha(canonicalAction.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)), 0, false, ""));
    }
    public synchronized void approve(Auth a, String operation, String digest,
                                      long now, long expiresAt) throws Exception {
        auth(a); live(now);
        if (state.phase()!=Phase.WAITING_APPROVAL || !state.operation().equals(operation)
                || !state.digest().equals(digest) || expiresAt<=now)
            throw new IllegalStateException("approval does not match pending action");
        save(next(Phase.READY, state.epoch(), "", 0, state.remaining(), operation,
                digest, Math.min(expiresAt,state.deadline()), false, ""));
    }
    public synchronized void dispatch(Auth a, String owner, long epoch,
                                      long now, String canonicalAction) throws Exception {
        auth(a); live(now); lease(owner,epoch,now);
        if (state.phase()!=Phase.RUNNING || state.approvalUntil()<=now || state.remaining()<=0
                || !state.digest().equals(sha(canonicalAction.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))))
            throw new IllegalStateException("dispatch rejected");
        // WAL intent first. Only after this method returns may caller contact tool.
        save(next(Phase.DISPATCHED,epoch,owner,state.leaseUntil(),state.remaining()-1,
                state.operation(),state.digest(),state.approvalUntil(),false,""));
    }
    public synchronized void complete(String owner,long epoch,long now,String receipt) throws Exception {
        lease(owner,epoch,now);
        if(state.phase()!=Phase.DISPATCHED || receipt.isBlank())
            throw new IllegalStateException("completion rejected");
        // Cancellation is not a claim that an already dispatched side effect vanished.
        save(next(Phase.SUCCEEDED,epoch,"",0,state.remaining(),state.operation(),
                state.digest(),state.approvalUntil(),state.cancelled(),receipt));
    }
    public synchronized void loseResponse() throws Exception {
        if(state.phase()!=Phase.DISPATCHED) throw new IllegalStateException("not dispatched");
        save(next(Phase.UNKNOWN,state.epoch(),"",0,state.remaining(),state.operation(),
                state.digest(),state.approvalUntil(),state.cancelled(),"requires reconciliation"));
    }
    public synchronized void requestCancel() throws Exception {
        if(terminal(state.phase())) return;
        Phase p = state.phase()==Phase.DISPATCHED || state.phase()==Phase.UNKNOWN
                ? Phase.UNKNOWN : Phase.CANCELLED;
        save(next(p,state.epoch(),"",0,state.remaining(),state.operation(),state.digest(),
                state.approvalUntil(),true,p==Phase.UNKNOWN?"cancelled; remote outcome unknown":"cancelled"));
    }
    public synchronized void reconcile(Auth a,RemoteState remote,String receipt) throws Exception {
        auth(a);
        if(state.phase()!=Phase.UNKNOWN) throw new IllegalStateException("not unknown");
        if(remote==RemoteState.UNKNOWN) return;
        if(receipt.isBlank()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("authoritative evidence required");
        Phase p = remote==RemoteState.APPLIED ? Phase.SUCCEEDED
                : (state.cancelled()?Phase.CANCELLED:Phase.FAILED);
        save(next(p,state.epoch(),"",0,state.remaining(),state.operation(),state.digest(),
                state.approvalUntil(),state.cancelled(),receipt));
    }
    public synchronized void recoverDispatched() throws Exception {
        if(state.phase()==Phase.DISPATCHED) loseResponse();
    }
    private void save(View v) throws Exception {
        if(poisoned) throw new IOException("journal is fail-closed after an IO error");
        byte[] payload=encode(v);
        if(payload.length>65_536) throw new IOException("state too large");
        String frame=Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(payload)+" "+sha(payload)+"\n";
        ByteBuffer bytes=StandardCharsets.US_ASCII.encode(frame);
        try {
            if(channel.size()+bytes.remaining()>8_388_608)throw new IOException("journal capacity");
            channel.position(channel.size());
            while(bytes.hasRemaining()) channel.write(bytes);
            channel.force(true);
        } catch(IOException e) { poisoned=true; throw e; }
        state=v;
    }
    private void recover() throws Exception {
        if(channel.size()>8_388_608) throw new IOException("demo journal limit");
        ByteBuffer b=ByteBuffer.allocate((int)channel.size());
        channel.position(0);
        while(b.hasRemaining()) if(channel.read(b)<0) throw new EOFException();
        String all=new String(b.array(),StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);
        if(!all.isEmpty() && !all.endsWith("\n")) throw new IOException("torn tail; reconcile before repair");
        long expected=0;
        for(String line:all.split("\n")) {
            if(line.isEmpty()) continue;
            String[] parts=line.split(" ",-1);
            if(parts.length!=2) throw new IOException("bad frame");
            byte[] raw=Base64.getDecoder().decode(parts[0]);
            if(!sha(raw).equals(parts[1])) throw new IOException("checksum mismatch");
            View v=decode(raw);
            if(v.sequence()!=expected++) throw new IOException("sequence gap");
            state=v;
        }
    }
    private static byte[] encode(View v) throws IOException {
        ByteArrayOutputStream bytes=new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        try(DataOutputStream out=new DataOutputStream(bytes)) {
            out.writeInt(1); out.writeLong(v.sequence());out.writeUTF(v.tenant());out.writeUTF(v.actor());
            out.writeUTF(v.phase().name());out.writeLong(v.epoch());out.writeUTF(v.owner());
            out.writeLong(v.leaseUntil());out.writeLong(v.deadline());out.writeInt(v.remaining());
            out.writeUTF(v.operation());out.writeUTF(v.digest());out.writeLong(v.approvalUntil());
            out.writeBoolean(v.cancelled());out.writeUTF(v.result());
        }
        return bytes.toByteArray();
    }
    private static View decode(byte[] bytes) throws IOException {
        try(DataInputStream in=new DataInputStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes))) {
            if(in.readInt()!=1) throw new IOException("unsupported schema");
            View v=new View(in.readLong(),in.readUTF(),in.readUTF(),Phase.valueOf(in.readUTF()),
                in.readLong(),in.readUTF(),in.readLong(),in.readLong(),in.readInt(),in.readUTF(),
                in.readUTF(),in.readLong(),in.readBoolean(),in.readUTF());
            if(in.available()!=0)throw new IOException("unexpected bytes");
            return v;
        }
    }
    public static String sha(byte[] raw) {
        try{return HexFormat.of().formatHex(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(raw));}
        catch(Exception e){throw new IllegalStateException(e);}
    }
    @Override public synchronized void close() throws IOException {
        IOException failure=null;
        try { if(lock.isValid())lock.release(); } catch(IOException e){failure=e;}
        try { channel.close(); } catch(IOException e){if(failure==null)failure=e;else failure.addSuppressed(e);}
        if(failure!=null)throw failure;
    }
}
```

### 有界并行 DAG：正确的 join 比开线程更重要

`BoundedDag` 的输入是只读、可中断的可信 `Callable<String>`。先检查重复节点、缺失依赖和环，才执行任何节点；入度归零进入 ready；完成后才释放下游；并发数和总期限有限。`CompletionService` 避免按提交顺序逐个 get 导致被早提交的慢任务挡住其他完成结果。

这是进程内调度器，非 durable 工作流。进程被杀后队列会丢，不能拿这个实现冒充第 6 节的分布式方案。`Future.cancel(true)` 只请求协作中断；不响应中断的任务可继续占线程，因此代码在关闭等待失败时显式报错。对不可信/不可中断执行，应使用独立进程和操作系统限制，本例不会创建外部进程。

线程池内部虽使用 JDK 默认工作队列，调度器只提交不超过 parallelism 的活动任务，不预先塞入整个图；节点总数与返回体仍需由接入层做业务限额。结果 Map 是完成结果，不是让任意 Worker 无锁修改的共享业务状态。

### 完整文件 `src/BoundedDag.java`

```Java
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

/** In-process, read-only teaching scheduler. Not a durable distributed scheduler. */
public final class BoundedDag {
    public record Node(String id, Set<String> dependencies, Callable<String> action) {
        public Node { dependencies=Set.copyOf(dependencies);Objects.requireNonNull(action); }
    }
    private record Result(String id,String value) {}
    public static Map<String,String> run(List<Node> input,int parallelism,long timeoutMillis)
            throws Exception {
        if(parallelism<1 || timeoutMillis<1 || timeoutMillis>60_000 || input.size()>1000)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("bounded inputs required");
        Map<String,Node> nodes=new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for(Node n:input) if(n.id().isBlank() || nodes.putIfAbsent(n.id(),n)!=null)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("duplicate or empty node");
        Map<String,Integer> indegree=new HashMap<>();
        Map<String,List<String>> children=new HashMap<>();
        for(Node n:input) {
            indegree.put(n.id(),n.dependencies().size());
            for(String d:n.dependencies()) {
                if(!nodes.containsKey(d)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("unknown dependency");
                children.computeIfAbsent(d,k->new ArrayList<>()).add(n.id());
            }
        }
        // Validate the entire DAG before any side effect or task submission.
        Map<String,Integer> degrees=new HashMap<>(indegree);
        ArrayDeque<String> check=new ArrayDeque<>();
        for(Node n:input) if(degrees.get(n.id())==0) check.add(n.id());
        int visited=0;
        while(!check.isEmpty()) {
            String id=check.remove();visited++;
            for(String child:children.getOrDefault(id,List.of()))
                if(degrees.compute(child,(k,v)->v-1)==0)check.add(child);
        }
        if(visited!=nodes.size()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("cycle");
        ArrayDeque<String> ready=new ArrayDeque<>();
        for(Node n:input)if(indegree.get(n.id())==0)ready.add(n.id());
        ExecutorService pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(parallelism);
        ExecutorCompletionService<Result> completions=new ExecutorCompletionService<>(pool);
        List<Future<Result>> futures=new ArrayList<>();
        Map<String,String> result=new LinkedHashMap<>();
        long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(timeoutMillis);
        int running=0;
        Throwable failure=null;
        try {
            while(result.size()<nodes.size()) {
                while(running<parallelism&&!ready.isEmpty()) {
                    if(deadline-System.nanoTime()<=0)throw new TimeoutException("before dispatch");
                    String id=ready.remove();Node n=nodes.get(id);
                    futures.add(completions.submit(()->new Result(id,n.action().call())));
                    running++;
                }
                long remaining=deadline-System.nanoTime();
                if(remaining<=0)throw new TimeoutException("DAG deadline");
                Future<Result> future=completions.poll(remaining,TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
                if(future==null)throw new TimeoutException("DAG deadline");
                Result done=future.get();running--; // Failure prevents downstream readiness.
                result.put(done.id(),Objects.requireNonNull(done.value()));
                for(String child:children.getOrDefault(done.id(),List.of()))
                    if(indegree.compute(child,(k,v)->v-1)==0)ready.add(child);
            }
            return Collections.unmodifiableMap(result);
        } catch(Exception|Error e) {
            failure=e;throw e;
        } finally {
            try {
                for(Future<Result> f:futures)if(!f.isDone())f.cancel(true);
                pool.shutdownNow();
                // Cooperative tasks only. This is not a hard kill or external rollback.
                if(!pool.awaitTermination(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS))
                    throw new IllegalStateException("worker ignored cancellation");
            } catch(Exception cleanup) {
                if(cleanup instanceof InterruptedException)Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                if(failure!=null)failure.addSuppressed(cleanup);else throw cleanup;
            } finally {
                if(failure instanceof InterruptedException)Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 原题变式：分批执行与保留指定终点的路径

任务依赖调度器常追问“哪些节点可同一批执行”。Kahn 算法每轮取当前全部零入度节点形成一个批次，再统一释放下一层。分批是依赖层次，不表示同批任务一定能同时占用资源；实际运行仍受并发配额限制。

另一原题给定 DAG、入度为零的起点集合和需保留的出度为零终点集合，要求删除不保留终点及其上游路径，又不能误删保留终点的共享路径。设 F 是从给定起点正向可达集合，B 是从保留终点沿反向边可达集合，保留 `F∩B` 及内部边即可。共享祖先能到保留终点就留下，不能按“碰到坏终点就删所有祖先”处理。原题候选人背景为 C\+\+；这里给出 Java 对应实现，未把语言来源改写成 Java 原题。

该实现允许任意显式端点并将零边路径算作可达，这是比原题更一般的接口；应用若要求端点必须为源/汇点，还需验证其入/出度。空起点或空保留集合返回空图。拓扑与双向遍历的图论部分为 `O(V+E)`；为结果稳定而使用排序结构/批次排序，实际实现还有排序开销，不能把这段代码无条件写成严格线性。空间为 `O(V+E)`。

### 完整文件 `src/DagAlgorithms.java`

```Java
import java.util.*;

public final class DagAlgorithms {
    private static Map<String,Set<String>> validate(Map<String,Set<String>> graph) {
        Map<String,Set<String>> copy=new TreeMap<>();
        graph.forEach((k,v)->copy.put(Objects.requireNonNull(k),Set.copyOf(v)));
        for(var targets:copy.values())if(!copy.keySet().containsAll(targets))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("unknown node");
        return copy;
    }
    /** Edges point from prerequisite to dependent. Stable lexicographic batches. */
    public static List<List<String>> batches(Map<String,Set<String>> graph) {
        Map<String,Set<String>> g=validate(graph);Map<String,Integer> degree=new TreeMap<>();
        g.keySet().forEach(n->degree.put(n,0));g.values().forEach(v->v.forEach(n->degree.merge(n,1,Integer::sum)));
        List<String> ready=new ArrayList<>();for(var entry:degree.entrySet())if(entry.getValue()==0)ready.add(entry.getKey());
        List<List<String>> result=new ArrayList<>();int count=0;
        while(!ready.isEmpty()) {
            result.add(List.copyOf(ready));count+=ready.size();List<String> next=new ArrayList<>();
            for(String n:ready)for(String to:g.get(n))if(degree.merge(to,-1,Integer::sum)==0)next.add(to);
            Collections.sort(next);ready=next;
        }
        if(count!=g.size())throw new IllegalArgumentException("cycle");return List.copyOf(result);
    }
    /** Keep exactly nodes on a path from any start to any end (zero-edge paths count). */
    public static Map<String,Set<String>> prune(Map<String,Set<String>> graph,Set<String> starts,Set<String> ends) {
        Map<String,Set<String>> g=validate(graph);batches(g);
        if(!g.keySet().containsAll(starts)||!g.keySet().containsAll(ends))throw new IllegalArgumentException("unknown endpoint");
        Map<String,Set<String>> reverse=new TreeMap<>();g.keySet().forEach(n->reverse.put(n,new TreeSet<>()));
        g.forEach((from,targets)->targets.forEach(to->reverse.get(to).add(from)));
        Set<String> keep=reachable(g,starts);keep.retainAll(reachable(reverse,ends));
        Map<String,Set<String>> output=new TreeMap<>();
        for(String n:keep){Set<String> edges=new TreeSet<>(g.get(n));edges.retainAll(keep);output.put(n,Set.copyOf(edges));}
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(output);
    }
    private static Set<String> reachable(Map<String,Set<String>> g,Set<String> roots) {
        Set<String> seen=new HashSet<>(roots);Deque<String> queue=new ArrayDeque<>(roots);
        while(!queue.isEmpty())for(String n:g.get(queue.remove()))if(seen.add(n))queue.add(n);
        return seen;
    }
}
```

## 30 Java 流事件与 Provider 用量的正确投影

**面试回答：** 网络解析、协议适配、UI 状态和业务执行分四层。以下代码接收已解码的 typed event，不打开网络、不解析任意 JSON、不自动执行工具。这样可独立测试交错参数、重复帧、缺口、终态和累计用量，避免把协议错误藏在 Controller 中。

### 最小工具参数流

`StreamProjection` 按 Run 与序号处理有界流，分 call ID 组装参数。同序号同载荷忽略，冲突重复或缺口拒绝；`TOOL_END` 只返回完整参数候选，还需真实 JSON/schema、权限和副作用策略。这个窄应用合同规定正常 END 前工具结果齐全，人工暂停使用另外的终态，不把流关闭当任务成功。

### 完整文件 `src/StreamProjection.java`

```Java
import java.util.*;

/** Typed event projection after provider/SSE parsing; deliberately no JSON parser. */
public final class StreamProjection {
    public enum Type { START, TEXT, TOOL_START, TOOL_DELTA, TOOL_END, TOOL_RESULT, END }
    public record Event(String run,long sequence,Type type,String call,String text) {}
    public record Call(String id,String arguments) {}
    private final String run;
    private final Map<Long,Event> seen=new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String,StringBuilder> partial=new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String,String> complete=new HashMap<>();
    private final Set<String> results=new HashSet<>();
    private final StringBuilder text=new StringBuilder();
    private long cursor=0;
    private boolean started,ended;
    public StreamProjection(String run){this.run=Objects.requireNonNull(run);}
    public synchronized Optional<Call> accept(Event e) {
        if(!run.equals(e.run()))throw new IllegalArgumentException("wrong run");
        Event previous=seen.get(e.sequence());
        if(previous!=null) {
            if(!previous.equals(e))throw new IllegalArgumentException("duplicate ID with different payload");
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        if(e.sequence()!=cursor+1 || seen.size()>=1000 || ended)
            throw new IllegalStateException("gap, limit, or terminal run");
        if(e.text()==null || e.text().length()>8192 || e.call()==null)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid frame");
        Optional<Call> output=Optional.empty();
        if(e.type()!=Type.START && !started)throw new IllegalStateException("missing start");
        switch(e.type()) {
            case START -> {if(started)throw new IllegalStateException("second start");started=true;}
            case TEXT -> {if(text.length()+e.text().length()>32768)throw new IllegalStateException("text limit");text.append(e.text());}
            case TOOL_START -> {
                if(e.call().isBlank()||partial.containsKey(e.call())||complete.containsKey(e.call()))
                    throw new IllegalStateException("call collision");
                partial.put(e.call(),new StringBuilder());
            }
            case TOOL_DELTA -> {
                StringBuilder p=partial.get(e.call());
                if(p==null || p.length()+e.text().length()>8192)throw new IllegalStateException("invalid delta");
                p.append(e.text());
            }
            case TOOL_END -> {
                StringBuilder p=partial.remove(e.call());
                if(p==null)throw new IllegalStateException("unknown call end");
                String args=p.toString();complete.put(e.call(),args);
                // A candidate only: execution still requires real parsing, schema, auth and budget.
                output=Optional.of(new Call(e.call(),args));
            }
            case TOOL_RESULT -> {
                if(!complete.containsKey(e.call()) || !results.add(e.call()))
                    throw new IllegalStateException("orphan or repeated result");
            }
            case END -> {
                if(!partial.isEmpty() || !results.containsAll(complete.keySet()))
                    throw new IllegalStateException("unfinished calls");
                ended=true;
            }
        }
        seen.put(e.sequence(),e);cursor=e.sequence();return output;
    }
    public synchronized long cursor(){return cursor;}
    public synchronized String text(){return text.toString();}
    public synchronized boolean ended(){return ended;}
}
```

### 面向前端的 `AgentEventState`

本例对文本 start/content/end、工具参数/result、状态 snapshot/delta 与 run 终态建模。序号来自服务端持久事件外壳，不是 AG\-UI 所有事件天然携带的字段。接入层先验证租户、Run、schema，扩展 chunk 并处理不在本枚举内的协议事件。字段长度/count 是教学限额，生产还需字节限额、总会话上限与事件归档。

状态更新先在副本完成全部 TEST/PUT/REMOVE，全部成功才替换；失败不推进游标。这里的平面键值 patch 是应用内部语义，不是直接接受标准 `StateDelta` 的通用 JSON Patch；生产若接收 RFC 6902，必须正确实现数组索引、Pointer 转义和各操作原子性，不能使用字符串替换代替。

成功、失败、等待人工分别展示，等待人工后通过授权入口建立后续运行；前端事件不能授予权限或证明资金/文件等外部提交成功。工具参数完整与工具结果完成是两个时刻。

### 完整文件 `src/AgentEventState.java`

```Java
import java.util.*;

/** A bounded application profile after AG-UI JSON parsing and chunk expansion.
 * UI only; it cannot approve an action or execute a tool. State is flat string data,
 * not a general RFC 6902 engine. IDs and sequences belong to our durable envelope.
 */
public final class AgentEventState {
    public enum Type { RUN_STARTED, TEXT_MESSAGE_START, TEXT_MESSAGE_CONTENT,
        TEXT_MESSAGE_END, TOOL_CALL_START, TOOL_CALL_ARGS, TOOL_CALL_END,
        TOOL_CALL_RESULT, STATE_SNAPSHOT, STATE_DELTA, RUN_FINISHED, RUN_ERROR }
    public enum Op { PUT, REMOVE, TEST }
    public record Change(Op op,String key,String value) {}
    public record Event(long seq,Type type,String id,String text,Map<String,String> snapshot,
                        List<Change> changes) {
        public Event {Objects.requireNonNull(type);Objects.requireNonNull(id);Objects.requireNonNull(text);
            snapshot=Map.copyOf(snapshot);changes=List.copyOf(changes);}
    }
    private final Map<Long,Event> seen=new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String,String> messages=new HashMap<>(),calls=new HashMap<>();
    private final Set<String> closedMessages=new HashSet<>(),closedCalls=new HashSet<>(),results=new HashSet<>();
    private Map<String,String> state=Map.of();
    private long cursor;
    private boolean started,terminal;
    private String outcome="NEW";
    public synchronized void accept(Event e) {
        Event duplicate=seen.get(e.seq());
        if(duplicate!=null){if(!duplicate.equals(e))throw new IllegalArgumentException("conflicting duplicate");return;}
        if(terminal||e.seq()!=cursor+1||seen.size()>=1000)throw new IllegalStateException("terminal, gap, or limit");
        if(e.text().length()>8192||e.id().length()>256||e.changes().size()>128||e.snapshot().size()>128)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("oversize frame");
        if(!started&&e.type()!=Type.RUN_STARTED)throw new IllegalStateException("missing run start");
        switch(e.type()) {
            case RUN_STARTED -> {if(started)throw new IllegalStateException("duplicate start");started=true;outcome="RUNNING";}
            case TEXT_MESSAGE_START -> {fresh(e.id());messages.put(e.id(),"");}
            case TEXT_MESSAGE_CONTENT -> append(messages,closedMessages,e.id(),e.text());
            case TEXT_MESSAGE_END -> close(messages,closedMessages,e.id());
            case TOOL_CALL_START -> {fresh(e.id());calls.put(e.id(),"");}
            case TOOL_CALL_ARGS -> append(calls,closedCalls,e.id(),e.text());
            case TOOL_CALL_END -> close(calls,closedCalls,e.id()); // arguments complete, not action success
            case TOOL_CALL_RESULT -> {if(!closedCalls.contains(e.id())||!results.add(e.id()))throw new IllegalStateException("orphan result");}
            case STATE_SNAPSHOT -> {checkState(e.snapshot());state=e.snapshot();}
            case STATE_DELTA -> {
                Map<String,String> next=new HashMap<>(state);
                for(Change c:e.changes()) {
                    if(c.key()==null||c.op()==null)throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid patch");
                    switch(c.op()) {
                        case PUT -> next.put(c.key(),Objects.requireNonNull(c.value()));
                        case REMOVE -> {if(!next.containsKey(c.key()))throw new IllegalStateException("missing key");next.remove(c.key());}
                        case TEST -> {if(!Objects.equals(next.get(c.key()),c.value())||!next.containsKey(c.key()))throw new IllegalStateException("test failed");}
                    }
                }
                checkState(next);state=Map.copyOf(next); // failed patch never changes existing state
            }
            case RUN_FINISHED -> {
                if(!e.text().equals("success")&&!e.text().equals("interrupt"))throw new IllegalArgumentException("unknown outcome");
                if(e.text().equals("success")&&(!closedMessages.containsAll(messages.keySet())||!results.containsAll(calls.keySet())))
                    throw new IllegalStateException("unfinished result");
                terminal=true;outcome=e.text().equals("success")?"SUCCESS":"INTERRUPTED";
            }
            case RUN_ERROR -> {terminal=true;outcome="ERROR";}
        }
        seen.put(e.seq(),e);cursor=e.seq();
    }
    private void fresh(String id){if(id.isBlank()||messages.containsKey(id)||calls.containsKey(id))throw new IllegalStateException("ID collision");}
    private static void append(Map<String,String> map,Set<String> closed,String id,String delta) {
        String old=map.get(id);if(old==null||closed.contains(id)||old.length()+delta.length()>32768)
            throw new IllegalStateException("unknown, closed, or oversized item");map.put(id,old+delta);
    }
    private static void close(Map<String,String> map,Set<String> closed,String id) {
        if(!map.containsKey(id)||!closed.add(id))throw new IllegalStateException("unknown or closed item");
    }
    private static void checkState(Map<String,String> s) {
        if(s.size()>128)throw new IllegalArgumentException("state limit");
        for(var e:s.entrySet())if(e.getKey().length()>256||e.getValue()==null||e.getValue().length()>8192)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("state entry limit");
    }
    public synchronized Map<String,String> state(){return Map.copyOf(state);}
    public synchronized String message(String id){return messages.get(id);}
    public synchronized String outcome(){return outcome;}
    public synchronized long cursor(){return cursor;}
}
```

### 用量快照与缺失状态

每个实际模型请求先 `begin(attemptId, provider)`，即使中途断流没有任何 usage，也留下 complete=false。adapter 把嵌套 usage 字段提取成受控键值映射；同一 attempt 的 partial 字段覆盖累计快照，不累加；不同真实 attempt 才加总。例子把缺字段数值展示为零，但 complete=false 明确表示下界/未确认，绝不能当“供应商确认零收费”。

先约定累计用量如何更新：这份代码只接受计数不下降的合同。供应商若发送修订型统计，adapter 必须另定义可审计的修订事件，不能为了通过检查随便取 max。usage 终态由对应 provider 的明确完成与完整计数共同判定；业务输出被拒绝或取消，也不代表已消耗费用退回。

再核对一帧内部的关系。重复、负值、子集超总数、缓存分区冲突和整数溢出都要检测。对 Chat、Responses 和 DeepSeek，同时给出输入、输出及 `total_tokens` 时，必须验证 `total_tokens`=输入\+输出；缺任何操作数就保留未确认，不能填零硬验。

DeepSeek 的 miss 即使命中字段缺失，也不能大于输入；命中和 miss 齐全时，两者之和应等于输入。命中字段与从 `prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens` 提取的别名也必须一致；这里的 `cached_tokens` 是 adapter 提取后的受控键，不是把任意嵌套字段全部摊平。

全部验证通过后才提交快照和事件序号。任何坏帧都不能污染已经确认的状态，否则后续即使拿到正确用量，也可能建立在错误水位上。

### 完整文件 `src/UsageLedger.java`

```Java
import java.util.*;

/** Provider JSON is parsed by a bounded adapter before reaching this class. */
public final class UsageLedger {
    public enum Provider { OPENAI_CHAT, OPENAI_RESPONSES, CLAUDE, DEEPSEEK }
    public record Snapshot(long input,long output,long cachedRead,long cacheWrite,
                           long reasoning,boolean complete) {}
    private record Frame(long sequence,Map<String,Long> fields,boolean terminal) {}
    private static final class Attempt {
        final Provider provider;
        final Map<String,Long> raw=new HashMap<>();
        final Map<Long,Frame> seen=new HashMap<>();
        long cursor;
        boolean complete;
        Attempt(Provider provider){this.provider=provider;}
    }
    private final Map<String,Attempt> attempts=new HashMap<>();
    public synchronized void begin(String id,Provider provider) {
        if(id==null||id.isBlank()||attempts.containsKey(id)||attempts.size()>=1000)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid or duplicate attempt");
        attempts.put(id,new Attempt(Objects.requireNonNull(provider)));
    }
    public synchronized Snapshot accept(String id,Provider provider,long sequence,
                                         Map<String,Long> patch,boolean terminal) {
        if(id==null||id.isBlank()||patch==null||patch.size()>32)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid usage envelope");
        Frame f=new Frame(sequence,Map.copyOf(patch),terminal);
        Attempt a=attempts.get(id);
        if(a==null){if(attempts.size()>=1000)throw new IllegalStateException("attempt limit");a=new Attempt(Objects.requireNonNull(provider));}
        if(a.provider!=provider)throw new IllegalArgumentException("provider changed within attempt");
        Frame old=a.seen.get(sequence);
        if(old!=null){if(!old.equals(f))throw new IllegalArgumentException("changed event");return normalize(provider,a.raw,a.complete);}
        if(a.complete||sequence!=a.cursor+1||a.seen.size()>=1000)
            throw new IllegalStateException("terminal, gap, or event limit");
        Map<String,Long> next=new HashMap<>(a.raw);
        for(var e:patch.entrySet()) {
            if(e.getValue()<0||e.getValue()<next.getOrDefault(e.getKey(),0L))
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("negative or decreasing cumulative usage");
            next.put(e.getKey(),e.getValue()); // cumulative snapshot, never +=
        }
        Snapshot result=normalize(provider,next,terminal); // validate before commit
        a.raw.clear();a.raw.putAll(next);a.complete=terminal;a.cursor=sequence;a.seen.put(sequence,f);
        attempts.put(id,a);return result;
    }
    private static long value(Map<String,Long> m,String k){return m.getOrDefault(k,0L);}
    private static Snapshot normalize(Provider p,Map<String,Long> m,boolean complete) {
        String in=p==Provider.OPENAI_RESPONSES||p==Provider.CLAUDE?"input_tokens":"prompt_tokens";
        String out=p==Provider.OPENAI_RESPONSES||p==Provider.CLAUDE?"output_tokens":"completion_tokens";
        if(complete&&(!m.containsKey(in)||!m.containsKey(out)))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("final usage lacks required totals");
        long input=value(m,in),output=value(m,out),read=0,write=0;
        if(p==Provider.CLAUDE){read=value(m,"cache_read_input_tokens");write=value(m,"cache_creation_input_tokens");input=Math.addExact(input,Math.addExact(read,write));}
        else if(p==Provider.DEEPSEEK){
            boolean hit=m.containsKey("prompt_cache_hit_tokens"),alias=m.containsKey("cached_tokens");
            read=hit?value(m,"prompt_cache_hit_tokens"):value(m,"cached_tokens");
            if(hit&&alias&&read!=value(m,"cached_tokens"))
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("cache aliases disagree");
            if(m.containsKey(in)&&m.containsKey("prompt_cache_miss_tokens")) {
                long miss=value(m,"prompt_cache_miss_tokens");
                if(miss>input)throw new IllegalArgumentException("cache misses exceed input");
                if((hit||alias)&&input!=Math.addExact(read,miss))
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("cache partition disagrees with input");
            }
        } else {read=value(m,"cached_tokens");write=value(m,"cache_write_tokens");}
        // Validate reported totals when all operands are known; omitted is not zero.
        if(p!=Provider.CLAUDE&&m.containsKey("total_tokens")&&m.containsKey(in)&&m.containsKey(out)
                &&value(m,"total_tokens")!=Math.addExact(input,output))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("reported total disagrees with input plus output");
        long reasoning=value(m,"reasoning_tokens");
        if(read>input||write>input||reasoning>output)throw new IllegalArgumentException("subset exceeds total");
        return new Snapshot(input,output,read,write,reasoning,complete);
    }
    public synchronized Snapshot total() {
        long i=0,o=0,r=0,w=0,t=0;boolean all=!attempts.isEmpty();
        for(Attempt a:attempts.values()){
            Snapshot s=normalize(a.provider,a.raw,a.complete);i=Math.addExact(i,s.input());o=Math.addExact(o,s.output());
            r=Math.addExact(r,s.cachedRead());w=Math.addExact(w,s.cacheWrite());t=Math.addExact(t,s.reasoning());all&=s.complete();
        }
        return new Snapshot(i,o,r,w,t,all);
    }
}
```

### 编程追问：怎样写一个 API 日志分析 CLI，并接入 CI 告警

先把日志口径固定，再做流式校验、分组聚合和明确的退出码；平均数算对了，不代表时间窗口、失败分母和缺失用量也算对了。

这里选择一个可运行的 CSV 输入合同：UTF\-8，表头依次为 timestamp、model、tokens、`latency_ms`、status。每行代表一条已经结束的 API 请求；重复行会重复计数，不会猜测请求 ID 或自动去重。重试如果逐次记录，这份报告统计的是尝试，不能拿它冒充用户任务成功率。上游需要先把自己的状态规范为 OK 或 ERROR；超时、取消是否算 ERROR，应在生产采集合同中确定并保持一致。tokens 是该行已知的总用量，空字段表示未知，数字 0 表示已知为零；不会用零填补缺失用量。`latency_ms` 是有限、非负的十进制毫秒数，接受 1–18 位整数和可选的 1–6 位小数，不接受指数形式；均值包含所有选中请求，包括 ERROR。

输入时间必须带 Z 或显式时区偏移，统一转换成 Instant。筛选区间采用左闭右开 \[from, to\)，可另按模型精确过滤；桶从 UTC 的 Unix epoch 对齐，窗口长度为整数秒。同一时刻的不同偏移写法进入同一个桶。逐条处理日志，同时产生总体、按模型、按窗口与模型三种汇总；输出按固定键排序。字段、记录和分组数量都有上限，超过限制直接失败，不靠丢弃异常行来维持一份看起来健康的报表。正式处理持续日志时还要固定输入快照或读取终点，并定义迟到数据和补算策略。

错误率等于 ERROR 行数除以该组请求总数，成功率用相同分母；空样本输出 N/A。阈值属于 \(0,1\]，最多接受 9 位小数；任何非空汇总行的错误率达到阈值就告警，等于阈值也算触发。比较使用未舍入的计数和阈值，报表中的六位小数只用于展示。生产里若要加入最小样本数、连续窗口或迟滞，必须明确修改告警合同，不能为了少报警悄悄改分母。

程序先完整校验和计算，再写同目录临时文件，成功后原子替换目标报表；输入文件与输出文件不能相同，硬链接也会拒绝。输出所有 CSV 单元格都加引号并转义双引号；可能被电子表格解释为公式的模型显示值加文本前缀，分组仍使用原始模型标识。模型名允许逗号和引号，但拒绝换行及其他控制字符；`model_display` 是展示字段，不应拿它回写模型配置。不会导出请求正文、凭据或原始错误消息。

退出码 0 表示有数据且未触发阈值，2 表示报表已生成且阈值触发，3 表示报表已生成但没有选中请求，1 表示参数、输入或 I/O 失败。CI 应保留这个退出码，将 2 交给已有的告警渠道，将 3 按缺数据处理；遇到 1 不得把上一次遗留 CSV 当作本次结果。stderr 只给出简洁状态，程序本身不发邮件、消息或网络请求。原子替换不等于已验证断电后的磁盘持久性，跨文件系统也不能套用这个保证。

示例输入：

```Plaintext
timestamp,model,tokens,latency_ms,status
2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,model-a,120,25,OK
2026-10-11T00:00:30Z,model-a,,80,ERROR
2026-10-11T00:01:00Z,model-b,0,12,OK
```

使用完整 JDK 17 或更新版本编译并运行：

```Bash
javac --release 17 -encoding UTF-8 -d out src/ApiLogCli.java tests/ApiLogCliTest.java
java -cp out ApiLogCliTest
java -cp out ApiLogCli --input requests.csv --output report.csv \
  --from 2026-10-11T00:00:00Z --to 2026-10-11T00:02:00Z \
  --window-seconds 60 --error-threshold 0.5 --max-groups 10000
```

上面的失败请求所在窗口触发 0\.5 阈值，因此退出码应为 2，而不是把它看成程序异常。测试还应覆盖等于阈值但显示舍入相同的相邻值、时区等价、窗口边界、空样本、未知 tokens、数值溢出、畸形 CSV、字段与分组上限、模型名公式注入以及失败时保留旧报表。完整实现和测试见紧随其后的 ApiLogCli\.java、ApiLogCliTest\.java。

### 完整文件 `src/ApiLogCli.java`

```Java
import java.io.*;
import java.math.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.util.*;

/** Offline request-log analysis. Never sends an external alert or invokes an API. */
public final class ApiLogCli {
    static final List<String> HEADER = List.of("timestamp", "model", "tokens", "latency_ms", "status");
    static final int MAX_FIELD = 65_536, MAX_RECORD = 1_048_576;
    record Config(Path input, Path output, Instant from, Instant to, long windowSeconds,
                  BigDecimal errorThreshold, int maxGroups, String model) {}
    record Event(Instant time, String model, Long tokens, BigDecimal latency, boolean ok) {}
    record Key(String scope, Instant window, String model) {}
    static final Key ALL = new Key("ALL", null, "");
    static final Comparator<Key> ORDER = Comparator.comparing(Key::scope)
            .thenComparing(Key::window, Comparator.nullsFirst(Comparator.naturalOrder()))
            .thenComparing(Key::model);
    static final class Stats {
        long requests, successes, knownTokenRows, tokens;
        BigDecimal latency = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        void add(Event e) {
            requests = Math.incrementExact(requests);
            if (e.ok) successes = Math.incrementExact(successes);
            if (e.tokens != null) {
                knownTokenRows = Math.incrementExact(knownTokenRows);
                tokens = Math.addExact(tokens, e.tokens);
            }
            latency = latency.add(e.latency);
        }
        long errors() { return requests - successes; }
        boolean breaches(BigDecimal threshold) {
            // Do not compare a rounded display percentage with the threshold.
            return requests != 0 && BigDecimal.valueOf(errors())
                    .compareTo(threshold.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(requests))) >= 0;
        }
    }

    /** Strict bounded CSV, accepting LF/CRLF and quoted commas/quotes/newlines. */
    static final class Csv implements AutoCloseable {
        private final PushbackReader in;
        private boolean first = true;
        Csv(Reader reader) { in = new PushbackReader(reader, 1); }
        List<String> next() throws IOException {
            List<String> row = new ArrayList<>();
            StringBuilder field = new StringBuilder();
            int state = 0, chars = 0; // 0=start, 1=plain, 2=quoted, 3=after quote
            boolean any = false;
            while (true) {
                int c = in.read();
                if (first) { first = false; if (c == '\uFEFF') c = in.read(); }
                if (c == -1) {
                    if (state == 2) throw new IOException("Unclosed CSV quote");
                    if (!any) return null;
                    addField(row, field); return row;
                }
                any = true;
                if (++chars > MAX_RECORD) throw new IOException("CSV record too long");
                if (state == 2) {
                    if (c == '"') state = 3;
                    else append(field, c);
                    continue;
                }
                if (state == 3 && c == '"') { append(field, c); state = 2; continue; }
                if (c == ',') { addField(row, field); state = 0; continue; }
                if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
                    if (c == '\r' && in.read() != '\n') throw new IOException("Bare CR record separator");
                    addField(row, field); return row;
                }
                if (state == 3) throw new IOException("Characters after closing CSV quote");
                if (c == '"') {
                    if (state != 0) throw new IOException("Quote in unquoted CSV field");
                    state = 2;
                } else { append(field, c); state = 1; }
            }
        }
        private static void append(StringBuilder field, int c) throws IOException {
            if (field.length() >= MAX_FIELD) throw new IOException("CSV field too long");
            field.append((char)c);
        }
        private static void addField(List<String> row, StringBuilder field) throws IOException {
            if (row.size() >= HEADER.size()) throw new IOException("Too many CSV columns");
            row.add(field.toString()); field.setLength(0);
        }
        public void close() throws IOException { in.close(); }
    }

    static Instant instant(String value) { return OffsetDateTime.parse(value).toInstant(); }
    static long nonnegativeLong(String value) {
        if (!value.matches("[0-9]{1,19}")) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid integer");
        return Long.parseLong(value);
    }
    static Event event(List<String> row) {
        if (row.size() != HEADER.size()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected five columns");
        String model = row.get(1);
        if (model.isBlank() || model.length() > 256 || model.codePoints().anyMatch(Character::isISOControl))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid model identifier");
        // Blank means unknown usage; numeric zero is known zero, not missing usage.
        Long tokens = row.get(2).isEmpty() ? null : nonnegativeLong(row.get(2));
        if (!row.get(3).matches("[0-9]{1,18}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?"))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid latency");
        String status = row.get(4);
        if (!status.equals("OK") && !status.equals("ERROR"))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Status must be OK or ERROR");
        return new Event(instant(row.get(0)), model, tokens, new BigDecimal(row.get(3)), status.equals("OK"));
    }

    static Config parse(String[] args) {
        Map<String, String> options = new HashMap<>();
        Set<String> allowed = Set.of("--input", "--output", "--from", "--to", "--window-seconds",
                "--error-threshold", "--max-groups", "--model");
        if (args.length % 2 != 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Options require values");
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2)
            if (!allowed.contains(args[i]) || options.putIfAbsent(args[i], args[i + 1]) != null)
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown or repeated option");
        for (String required : List.of("--input", "--output", "--from", "--to", "--window-seconds", "--error-threshold"))
            if (!options.containsKey(required)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing " + required);
        Instant from = instant(options.get("--from")), to = instant(options.get("--to"));
        if (!from.isBefore(to)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("from must precede to");
        long seconds = nonnegativeLong(options.get("--window-seconds"));
        if (seconds < 1 || seconds > 86_400) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Window must be 1..86400 seconds");
        String thresholdText = options.get("--error-threshold");
        if (!thresholdText.matches("[0-9]{1,2}(\\.[0-9]{1,9})?"))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid threshold representation");
        BigDecimal threshold = new BigDecimal(thresholdText);
        if (threshold.signum() <= 0 || threshold.compareTo(BigDecimal.ONE) > 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Threshold must be >0 and <=1");
        long limit = nonnegativeLong(options.getOrDefault("--max-groups", "10000"));
        if (limit < 3 || limit > 1_000_000) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Group limit must be 3..1000000");
        String model = options.get("--model");
        if (model != null && (model.isBlank() || model.length() > 256 || model.codePoints().anyMatch(Character::isISOControl)))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid model filter");
        return new Config(Path.of(options.get("--input")).toAbsolutePath().normalize(),
                Path.of(options.get("--output")).toAbsolutePath().normalize(),
                from, to, seconds, threshold, (int)limit, model);
    }

    static NavigableMap<Key, Stats> analyze(Config config) throws IOException {
        NavigableMap<Key, Stats> groups = new TreeMap<>(ORDER); groups.put(ALL, new Stats());
        try (Csv csv = new Csv(Files.newBufferedReader(config.input, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
            if (!HEADER.equals(csv.next())) throw new IOException("Unexpected CSV header/order");
            List<String> row; long record = 1;
            while ((row = csv.next()) != null) {
                record = Math.incrementExact(record);
                Event e;
                try { e = event(row); }
                catch (RuntimeException invalid) { throw new IOException("Invalid request record " + record, invalid); }
                // Validate even excluded rows: filtering must not hide malformed input.
                if (e.time.isBefore(config.from) || !e.time.isBefore(config.to)
                        || (config.model != null && !config.model.equals(e.model))) continue;
                Instant window = Instant.ofEpochSecond(Math.multiplyExact(
                        Math.floorDiv(e.time.getEpochSecond(), config.windowSeconds), config.windowSeconds));
                for (Key key : List.of(ALL, new Key("MODEL", null, e.model), new Key("WINDOW_MODEL", window, e.model))) {
                    Stats stats = groups.get(key);
                    if (stats == null) {
                        if (groups.size() >= config.maxGroups) throw new IOException("Distinct group limit exceeded");
                        stats = new Stats(); groups.put(key, stats);
                    }
                    stats.add(e);
                }
            }
        }
        return groups;
    }

    static String ratio(long numerator, long denominator) {
        return denominator == 0 ? "N/A" : BigDecimal.valueOf(numerator)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(denominator), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).toPlainString();
    }
    static String modelDisplay(String raw) {
        // The report is for people/spreadsheets, not a lossless identifier round trip.
        String leading = raw.stripLeading();
        return !leading.isEmpty() && "=+-@".indexOf(leading.charAt(0)) >= 0 ? "'" + raw : raw;
    }
    static String cell(String text) { return "\"" + text.replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\""; }
    static void writeRow(Writer writer, List<String> values) throws IOException {
        for (int i = 0; i < values.size(); i++) {
            if (i != 0) writer.write(','); writer.write(cell(values.get(i)));
        }
        writer.write('\n');
    }
    static void report(Config config, NavigableMap<Key, Stats> groups) throws IOException {
        if (config.input.equals(config.output) || (Files.exists(config.output) && Files.isSameFile(config.input, config.output)))
            throw new IOException("Input and output must be different files");
        Path temporary = Files.createTempFile(config.output.getParent(), ".api-log-", ".tmp");
        try {
            try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(temporary, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                writeRow(writer, List.of("scope", "window_start_utc", "model_display", "requests", "successes", "errors",
                        "success_rate", "error_rate", "mean_latency_ms", "known_token_rows", "unknown_token_rows", "known_tokens", "breach"));
                for (Map.Entry<Key, Stats> entry : groups.entrySet()) {
                    Key k = entry.getKey(); Stats s = entry.getValue();
                    writeRow(writer, List.of(k.scope, k.window == null ? "" : k.window.toString(), modelDisplay(k.model),
                            Long.toString(s.requests), Long.toString(s.successes), Long.toString(s.errors()),
                            ratio(s.successes, s.requests), ratio(s.errors(), s.requests),
                            s.requests == 0 ? "N/A" : s.latency.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(s.requests), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).toPlainString(),
                            Long.toString(s.knownTokenRows), Long.toString(s.requests - s.knownTokenRows),
                            s.knownTokenRows == 0 ? "N/A" : Long.toString(s.tokens), Boolean.toString(s.breaches(config.errorThreshold))));
                }
            }
            // Unsupported atomic replacement is an error; no non-atomic silent fallback.
            Files.move(temporary, config.output, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException | Error failure) {
            try { Files.deleteIfExists(temporary); }
            catch (IOException cleanup) { if (cleanup != failure) failure.addSuppressed(cleanup); }
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    /** 0=pass, 1=invalid input/I/O, 2=threshold breached, 3=no selected requests. */
    static int run(String[] args, PrintStream err) {
        try {
            Config config = parse(args);
            NavigableMap<Key, Stats> groups = analyze(config);
            report(config, groups);
            if (groups.get(ALL).requests == 0) { err.println("NO_DATA: no selected requests"); return 3; }
            long breaches = groups.values().stream().filter(s -> s.breaches(config.errorThreshold)).count();
            if (breaches != 0) { err.println("ALERT: error threshold reached in " + breaches + " aggregate rows"); return 2; }
            err.println("PASS: report written"); return 0;
        } catch (Exception failure) {
            // Do not echo raw log fields or exception messages containing sensitive input.
            err.println("INPUT_OR_IO_ERROR: " + failure.getClass().getSimpleName()); return 1;
        }
    }
    public static void main(String[] args) { System.exit(run(args, System.err)); }
}
```

### 完整文件 `tests/ApiLogCliTest.java`

```Java
import java.io.*;
import java.math.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.util.*;

public final class ApiLogCliTest {
    static int checks;
    static void check(boolean condition, String name) {
        if (!condition) throw new AssertionError(name);
        checks++;
    }
    interface Action { void run() throws Exception; }
    static void rejects(Action action, String name) throws Exception {
        try { action.run(); } catch (IOException | IllegalArgumentException | ArithmeticException expected) {
            checks++; return;
        }
        throw new AssertionError(name);
    }
    static String[] args(Path in, Path out, String threshold) {
        return new String[]{"--input",in.toString(),"--output",out.toString(),"--from","2026-10-11T00:00:00Z",
                "--to","2026-10-11T00:02:00Z","--window-seconds","60","--error-threshold",threshold};
    }
    static String[] append(String[] original, String... tail) {
        String[] both = Arrays.copyOf(original, original.length + tail.length);
        System.arraycopy(tail,0,both,original.length,tail.length); return both;
    }
    static String[] option(String[] original, String key, String value) {
        String[] copy = original.clone();
        for (int i = 0; i < copy.length; i += 2) if (copy[i].equals(key)) copy[i+1] = value;
        return copy;
    }
    static int run(String[] args) {
        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        return ApiLogCli.run(args, new PrintStream(captured, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }
    static final String HEADER = "timestamp,model,tokens,latency_ms,status\n";
    public static void main(String[] unused) throws Exception {
        Path dir = Files.createTempDirectory("api-log-author-tests-");
        try {
            Path in = dir.resolve("requests.csv"), out = dir.resolve("report.csv");
            String fixture = HEADER + "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,\"model,a\",10,10,OK\n"
                    + "2026-10-11T01:00:30+01:00,\"model,a\",,30,ERROR\n"
                    + "2026-10-11T00:01:00Z,b,0,5,OK\n"
                    + "2026-10-11T00:02:00Z,b,999,1000,ERROR\n";
            Files.writeString(in, fixture);
            String[] args = args(in,out,"0.5");
            var groups = ApiLogCli.analyze(ApiLogCli.parse(args));
            var all = groups.get(ApiLogCli.ALL);
            check(all.requests == 3 && all.successes == 2 && all.errors() == 1, "half-open range denominator");
            check(all.knownTokenRows == 2 && all.tokens == 10, "unknown tokens distinct from zero");
            check(all.latency.compareTo(new BigDecimal("45")) == 0, "all selected latency sum");
            check(groups.size() == 5, "total, model, UTC window-model groups");
            check(groups.get(new ApiLogCli.Key("WINDOW_MODEL", Instant.parse("2026-10-11T00:00:00Z"),"model,a")).requests == 2,
                    "offset equivalence and UTC window boundary");
            check(run(args) == 2, "threshold equality fails CI");
            String report = Files.readString(out);
            check(report.contains("\"15.000000\",\"2\",\"1\",\"10\""), "mean and usage in report");
            check(report.contains("\"model,a\""), "comma model quoted");
            check(run(args) == 2 && Files.readString(out).equals(report), "deterministic bytes and replacement");
            check(run(option(args,"--error-threshold","0.500000001")) == 0, "threshold compared before rounding");
            check(run(append(args,"--model","b")) == 0, "model filter");
            check(run(append(args,"--model","missing")) == 3, "empty selection separate CI status");
            check(Files.readString(out).contains("\"N/A\",\"N/A\",\"N/A\""), "empty rates are N/A");
            for (String value : List.of("0","-0.1","1.1","NaN","1e-1","","0.1234567890"))
                check(run(option(args,"--error-threshold",value)) == 1, "bad threshold " + value);
            check(run(option(args,"--from","2026-10-11T00:00:00")) == 1, "timezone required");
            check(run(option(args,"--from","2026-10-11T00:02:00Z")) == 1, "empty filter interval rejected");
            check(run(append(args,"--input",in.toString())) == 1, "duplicate option");
            check(run(append(args,"--unknown","x")) == 1, "unknown option");
            check(run(append(args,"--max-groups","3")) == 1, "group cap stops unbounded cardinality");
            Files.writeString(out, "preserve-before-input-error");
            Files.writeString(in, fixture + "2026-10-11T00:03:00Z,b,0,1,TYPO\n");
            check(run(args) == 1 && Files.readString(out).equals("preserve-before-input-error"), "invalid excluded row still fails without report overwrite");
            for (String row : List.of(
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,-1,1,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,9223372036854775808,1,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,-1,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,NaN,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,Infinity,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,1e3,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,1,ok",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,,1,1,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,\"multi\nline\",1,1,OK",
                    "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,1,1,OK,extra")) {
                Files.writeString(in,HEADER+row+"\n"); check(run(args) == 1,"invalid request contract");
            }
            Files.writeString(in, HEADER + "2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,m,9223372036854775807,1,OK\n"
                    + "2026-10-11T00:00:01Z,m,1,1,OK\n");
            check(run(args) == 1, "aggregate token overflow rejected");
            Files.writeString(in,HEADER+"2026-10-11T00:00:00Z,\"=SUM(1,2)\",,1,OK\n");
            check(run(args) == 0 && Files.readString(out).contains("\"'=SUM(1,2)\""), "spreadsheet formula prefix");
            check(ApiLogCli.modelDisplay("   +danger").equals("'   +danger"), "leading whitespace formula prefix");
            check(ApiLogCli.cell("a\"b").equals("\"a\"\"b\""), "quote escaping");
            try (var csv = new ApiLogCli.Csv(new StringReader("\uFEFF\"a,b\",\"x\"\"y\",\"multi\nline\"\r\n"))) {
                check(csv.next().equals(List.of("a,b","x\"y","multi\nline")) && csv.next() == null,"CSV syntax multiline and BOM");
            }
            for (String bad : List.of("\"unclosed", "a\"b,c", "\"a\"tail", "a\rb", "a".repeat(ApiLogCli.MAX_FIELD+1)))
                rejects(() -> { try (var csv = new ApiLogCli.Csv(new StringReader(bad))) { csv.next(); } },"bad CSV");
            try (var csv = new ApiLogCli.Csv(new StringReader("a,b,"))) {
                check(csv.next().equals(List.of("a","b","")) && csv.next() == null,"trailing empty field at EOF");
            }
            Files.writeString(in,HEADER);
            check(run(args) == 3,"header-only input");
            Files.writeString(in,fixture);
            check(run(option(args,"--output",in.toString())) == 1 && Files.readString(in).equals(fixture),"same file protected");
            Path hard = dir.resolve("same-hardlink.csv"); Files.createLink(hard,in);
            check(run(option(args,"--output",hard.toString())) == 1 && Files.readString(in).equals(fixture),"hard-linked input protected");
            Files.write(in,new byte[]{(byte)0xC3,(byte)0x28});
            check(run(args) == 1,"invalid UTF8 rejected");
            ApiLogCli.Stats overflow = new ApiLogCli.Stats(); overflow.requests = Long.MAX_VALUE;
            rejects(() -> overflow.add(new ApiLogCli.Event(Instant.EPOCH,"m",null,BigDecimal.ZERO,true)),"request overflow");
            System.out.println("PASS " + checks + " author checks");
        } finally {
            try (var files = Files.walk(dir)) {
                for (Path path : files.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).toList()) Files.deleteIfExists(path);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 同题换问与连续追问



- 设计一个 CLI 工具，用于分析存储 api 请求的日志文件，里面包含请求时间、使用模型、token用量、耗时、执行状态等信息。

    - 需要能够：1、计算基础口径（平均耗时、成功率、使用模型统计等）2、按照时间窗口、模型进行分析 3、实现 /CI 监测，信息落到 csv 文件中，错误率到达红线时能进行告警

## 31 用故障测试证明边界 而不是只演示成功路径

**面试回答：** 我按失败窗口构造测试，并使用与实现不同的 oracle。运行可恢复状态机要测“写意图前、写后调用前、远端成功后回执前、回执已提交响应前”；并行图测环、缺依赖、失败父节点和超时；协议测重复、乱序、半截参数和缺最终用量。测试验证本地合同，不替代分布式环境或真实供应商兼容测试。

### 独立断言检查什么

`RuntimeAdversarialTest` 共 26 个命名测试，检查权限/审批/租约边界、重启、UNKNOWN、取消、文件损坏、DAG 和工具流。`ProtocolAndGraphTest` 共 16 个命名测试，其中一个用固定随机种子生成 200 个 DAG，以 Floyd 可达矩阵作为独立 oracle，核对裁剪与批次边关系；还覆盖三家 token 口径、缺失用量、报告总数冲突、DeepSeek 缓存子集/别名、部分字段延迟校验及坏帧不提交、AG\-UI 应用投影。

`ApiLogCliTest` 另有 52 个检查，覆盖筛选、时间窗口、未知用量、精确阈值、CSV 与输入/输出保护；检查数与前述命名测试数分别报告，不混成同一统计口径。

Runtime、Protocol 与 CLI 测试使用本地临时文件和进程内任务；第 24 章校验器的 fixture 测试还启动本节完整列出的受控子 JVM，不执行仓库脚本或真实扫描器。它们不使用真实 broker、数据库、Provider 或业务副作用。Runtime 的租约时钟由测试显式推进，deadline/协作取消使用受限真实等待；进程 fixture 则用有截止时间的就绪信号与退出检查验证清理。运行时使用 `-ea`，测试本身另有显式断言和异常检查。

### 完整文件 `tests/RuntimeAdversarialTest.java`

```Java
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

public final class RuntimeAdversarialTest {
    private static int passed;
    private static final DurableRuntime.Auth YES=new DurableRuntime.Auth("tenant-a","user-a",true);
    private static final String ACTION="set-limit|resource=module-c|expectedVersion=7|limit=20";
    @FunctionalInterface interface Test {void run()throws Exception;}
    static void test(String name,Test t)throws Exception{t.run();passed++;System.out.println("PASS "+name);}
    static void check(boolean b){if(!b)throw new AssertionError();}
    static void rejects(Test t)throws Exception{
        try{t.run();}catch(SecurityException|IllegalStateException|IllegalArgumentException|java.io.IOException e){return;}
        throw new AssertionError("expected rejection");
    }
    static DurableRuntime fresh(Path p)throws Exception{return new DurableRuntime(p,"tenant-a","user-a",1000,2);}
    static long prepare(DurableRuntime r)throws Exception{
        long e=r.claim("worker-a",0,200);r.requestApproval("worker-a",e,1,"op-1",ACTION);
        r.approve(YES,"op-1",r.view().digest(),2,400);return r.claim("worker-b",3,200);
    }
    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception {
        Path dir=Files.createTempDirectory("runtime-teaching-");
        try {
            test("approval survives restart",()->{
                Path p=dir.resolve("approval.log");String digest;
                try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){long e=r.claim("a",0,50);r.requestApproval("a",e,1,"op",ACTION);digest=r.view().digest();}
                try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.WAITING_APPROVAL);r.approve(YES,"op",digest,2,80);check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.READY);}
            });
            test("two claimants cannot own one epoch",()->{
                try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("claim.log"))){
                    ExecutorService pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);AtomicInteger wins=new AtomicInteger();
                    try{List<Future<?>> fs=new ArrayList<>();for(int i=0;i<2;i++){String name="w"+i;fs.add(pool.submit(()->{try{r.claim(name,0,50);wins.incrementAndGet();}catch(IllegalStateException expected){}catch(Exception e){throw new RuntimeException(e);}}));}for(Future<?> f:fs)f.get();check(wins.get()==1);}finally{pool.shutdownNow();check(pool.awaitTermination(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS));}
                }
            });
            test("expired owner fenced",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("fence.log"))){long old=r.claim("old",0,10);long newer=r.claim("new",11,30);check(newer>old);rejects(()->r.heartbeat("old",old,12,10));}});
            test("lease boundary is expired",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("boundary.log"))){long e=r.claim("w",0,10);rejects(()->r.heartbeat("w",e,10,10));}});
            test("cross tenant approval denied",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("tenant.log"))){long e=r.claim("w",0,10);r.requestApproval("w",e,1,"op",ACTION);rejects(()->r.approve(new DurableRuntime.Auth("tenant-b","user-a",true),"op",r.view().digest(),2,20));}});
            test("approval bound to digest and operation",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("digest.log"))){long e=r.claim("w",0,10);r.requestApproval("w",e,1,"op",ACTION);rejects(()->r.approve(YES,"other",r.view().digest(),2,20));rejects(()->r.approve(YES,"op","bad",2,20));}});
            test("expired approval cannot dispatch",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("expiry.log"))){long e=prepare(r);r.heartbeat("worker-b",e,100,500);rejects(()->r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,400,ACTION));}});
            test("revocation checked at dispatch",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("revoke.log"))){long e=prepare(r);rejects(()->r.dispatch(new DurableRuntime.Auth("tenant-a","user-a",false),"worker-b",e,4,ACTION));check(r.view().remaining()==2);}});
            test("changed action cannot reuse approval",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("change.log"))){long e=prepare(r);rejects(()->r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,4,ACTION+"0"));}});
            test("dispatch is durable and never blindly replayed",()->{
                Path p=dir.resolve("unknown.log");try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){long e=prepare(r);r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,4,ACTION);}
                try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){r.recoverDispatched();check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.UNKNOWN);check(r.view().remaining()==1);rejects(()->r.claim("new",500,20));r.reconcile(YES,DurableRuntime.RemoteState.APPLIED,"remote-receipt-1");check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.SUCCEEDED);}
            });
            test("cancellation after dispatch retains uncertainty",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("cancel.log"))){long e=prepare(r);r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,4,ACTION);r.requestCancel();check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.UNKNOWN);r.reconcile(YES,DurableRuntime.RemoteState.APPLIED,"receipt");check(r.view().cancelled());check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.SUCCEEDED);}});
            test("cancel before dispatch means no tool",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("pre-cancel.log"))){long e=prepare(r);r.requestCancel();rejects(()->r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,4,ACTION));check(r.view().phase()==DurableRuntime.Phase.CANCELLED);}});
            test("deadline forbids claim",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("deadline.log"))){rejects(()->r.claim("a",1000,50));}});
            test("duplicate completion cannot advance sequence",()->{try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(dir.resolve("done.log"))){long e=prepare(r);r.dispatch(YES,"worker-b",e,4,ACTION);r.complete("worker-b",e,5,"receipt");long seq=r.view().sequence();rejects(()->r.complete("worker-b",e,6,"receipt"));check(r.view().sequence()==seq);}});
            test("single local writer lock",()->{Path p=dir.resolve("lock.log");try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){try(DurableRuntime ignored=fresh(p)){throw new AssertionError();}catch(java.nio.channels.OverlappingFileLockException expected){}}});
            test("torn journal fails closed",()->{Path p=dir.resolve("torn.log");try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){}Files.writeString(p,"partial",StandardOpenOption.APPEND);rejects(()->{try(DurableRuntime ignored=fresh(p)){} });});
            test("checksum corruption fails closed",()->{Path p=dir.resolve("corrupt.log");try(DurableRuntime r=fresh(p)){}byte[] data=Files.readAllBytes(p);data[5]=data[5]=='A'?(byte)'B':(byte)'A';Files.write(p,data);rejects(()->{try(DurableRuntime ignored=fresh(p)){} });});
            test("DAG join waits for both independent parents",()->{Set<String> done=ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();Map<String,String> r=BoundedDag.run(List.of(new BoundedDag.Node("a",Set.of(),()->{done.add("a");return "A";}),new BoundedDag.Node("b",Set.of(),()->{done.add("b");return "B";}),new BoundedDag.Node("join",Set.of("a","b"),()->{check(done.size()==2);return "AB";})),2,1000);check(r.get("join").equals("AB"));});
            test("DAG rejects cycle before any execution",()->{AtomicInteger calls=new AtomicInteger();rejects(()->BoundedDag.run(List.of(new BoundedDag.Node("a",Set.of("b"),()->{calls.incrementAndGet();return "";}),new BoundedDag.Node("b",Set.of("a"),()->"")),2,1000));check(calls.get()==0);});
            test("DAG rejects unknown dependency",()->rejects(()->BoundedDag.run(List.of(new BoundedDag.Node("a",Set.of("missing"),()->"")),1,1000)));
            test("failed dependency cannot run child",()->{AtomicInteger child=new AtomicInteger();try{BoundedDag.run(List.of(new BoundedDag.Node("a",Set.of(),()->{throw new IllegalStateException("boom");}),new BoundedDag.Node("b",Set.of("a"),()->{child.incrementAndGet();return "";})),1,1000);throw new AssertionError();}catch(ExecutionException expected){}check(child.get()==0);});
            test("DAG deadline cancels cooperative work",()->{try{BoundedDag.run(List.of(new BoundedDag.Node("wait",Set.of(),()->{new CountDownLatch(1).await();return "";})),1,20);throw new AssertionError();}catch(TimeoutException expected){}});
            test("stream duplicates do not duplicate text",()->{StreamProjection s=new StreamProjection("r");s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",1,StreamProjection.Type.START,"",""));var e=new StreamProjection.Event("r",2,StreamProjection.Type.TEXT,"","你");s.accept(e);s.accept(e);check(s.text().equals("你"));});
            test("stream gaps and cross run are rejected",()->{StreamProjection s=new StreamProjection("r");rejects(()->s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",2,StreamProjection.Type.START,"","")));rejects(()->s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("other",1,StreamProjection.Type.START,"","")));});
            test("partial tool arguments are not executable",()->{StreamProjection s=new StreamProjection("r");s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",1,StreamProjection.Type.START,"",""));s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",2,StreamProjection.Type.TOOL_START,"c",""));check(s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",3,StreamProjection.Type.TOOL_DELTA,"c","{\"x\":" )).isEmpty());check(s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",4,StreamProjection.Type.TOOL_DELTA,"c","1}")).isEmpty());check(s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",5,StreamProjection.Type.TOOL_END,"c","")).orElseThrow().arguments().equals("{\"x\":1}"));rejects(()->s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",6,StreamProjection.Type.END,"","")));s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",6,StreamProjection.Type.TOOL_RESULT,"c","ok"));s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",7,StreamProjection.Type.END,"",""));check(s.ended());});
            test("same stream ID different data rejected",()->{StreamProjection s=new StreamProjection("r");s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",1,StreamProjection.Type.START,"",""));rejects(()->s.accept(new StreamProjection.Event("r",1,StreamProjection.Type.START,"","different")));});
            System.out.println("TOTAL "+passed+" passed");
        } finally {try(var walk=Files.walk(dir)){for(Path p:walk.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).toList())Files.delete(p);}}
    }
}
```
